.topic 1000

e-Bility and Bookscan are Bookscan PTY LTDs point of sale and inventory management solution for bookshops, music, stationery and other retail or wholesale stores.




Click on the link for details on contacting Bookscan Pty Ltd.

Help files version 6.00.02

.topic 2270
This section will help you with an outline on doing some tasks within e-Bility which may require setups to be done in various locations. This will help with seeing the bigger picture on how a feature in e-Bility works.

.topic 2450
The follow steps are applicable for those who are upgrading to a new version of Bookscan.



Files used for Update

In your L:\e-Bilitydownloads\ folder you will need to place the following files;
·	Bookscan6.nn.nn.exe – Bookscan installation file
·	Reports.n.nn.zip – Bookscan report forms (in a zip file if updating the reports as well.)
n = number (these numbers will change)
These files may be emailed or a link given for you to download. To get these contact support (support@bookscan.com.au 1300 766 905 or 02 8999 1195). Click on these links to go to the help pages for Downloading and Saving an attachment from an email.

Updatling Bookscan

The installation of Bookscan will be carried out on each machine; the first machine to do with an upgrade is on the server machine. Users need to ensure Bookscan and e-Comms are closed on all machines, including the server before doing the install.
Open Computer -> L:\ -> e-Bilitydownloads  (Where L:\ is the mapped network drive.)
Run the Bookscan.nn.nn.exe (The newest version, higher numbers are newer) by double clicking on the file.


Click Yes button

Click Next button

Select 'I accept the agreement', then click Next button.

Leave it as the default location (So when installed it will be located at C:\Program Files\Bookscan\ on all computers)
Click the Next button

Click the Next button

Click the Next button (leave the defaults, so it will create a desktop icon)


To continue with the installation click the Install button.


Do not click the Cancel button, allow it to continue.


Click Finish button when done.

Note: When the first copy of the new version of Bookscan is started, it will trigger a structure update to run.  You must allow this to complete before starting Bookscan on another computer as per Post Installation.  Once this has completed, you can update & start Bookscan on all other PCs.


Post Installation - Structure Update / Re-index

As part of a new version, the software will now set up the databases. What this will do is, if the files required to store the information are not there, it will create them, if it is and new fields have been added, it will do so at this time.  Without doing this, the program may not work correctly.
This process will only take place on the first machine to run a new version. It is important that the version not be run on any other machine until the process is complete (as the other computers will not know this is running, and run it themselves, which we don't want.)
The following is an example of what will be displayed somewhere on your desktop, and it will constantly change the text that shows on it.


If you get an update error message, please contact the Help Desk immediately before continuing.
If you are prompted with an error message with a 'Retry' option, try clicking it and seeing if it continues. If you are not, but are prompted with an 'Ignore', try that, note down beforehand what was shown in the little window displayed above, and the error message before doing this. (You will still need to contact help desk)
Once the database files are set up, Bookscan is installed.
The system will then automatically re-index all databases. The re-index window will look like this;


When done, it will display a message indicating that if there were any errors/issues to contact support, click the messages OK button.

If no problems move onto installing Bookscan on the next computer, Bookscan is ready to use on the server.

New Stores – Additional steps
New users once this is done for the first time will be prompted to fill in their stores details. Fill in as much as you can, then click the OK button to save. (The details can be amended later, refer to the help files [F1 key in Bookscan])

Please call the Help Desk at Barcode Solutions on +61 2  9683 6999, facsimile +61 2 9683 6955 or email support@barcode.com.au if you require assistance.


.topic 2280

Setting up your store to Electronic Order / receive invoices

To use the Pacsteam service for electronic ordering, firstly you will need to ensure that you are setup with a Pacstream account. If you do not have one, you will need to contact ECN Group. You can always contact Pacstream at support@pacstream.com.au. Then fill in, step by step the following parts of Bookscan so that when you wish to use it, it is ready to go. You will also need to make sure ECN group and the suppliers you wish to deal with using Pacstream are aware you will be ordering using this method.


Step 1: System Options

From the main menu of Bookscan, go to Utilities-->System Options. On the Licensee tab, complete the SAN field with your san number.   (If you dont know this, contact ECN group as mentioned above)
Click OK button to save the change.

Note: Use of the 'Alternate SAN' field should only be used in consultation with Barcode solutions, as there are special setups that you will need to have done with ECN group.


Step 3: Suppliers

For each supplier that you are going to be using Pacstream to order through, you will need to set each of them up for this. For each one of these suppliers, do the following;

1.   From the main menu of Bookscan open Tables-->Supplier Master
2.   Go to the suppliers record (using the search or by any other means). e.g. UNIT for United Book Distributers	
3.   Open the Address tab. 	
4.   Select 'PACSTREAM' from the EDI drop-down list.	
5.   Enter the suppliers SAN number in the SAN field, e.g. 	9013849
6.   Repeat for each pacstream supplier. 	

Note: If you do not have the supplier correctly setup with the SAN number that they will be using (Some suppliers have 2 SAN numbers, ensure you use the one they do for Electronic invoicing), there can be issues when receiving electronic invoices.

Step 4: e-Comms

e-Comms is the program which is what sends through the electronic orders to Pacstream and also retrieves the electronic invoices back. e-Comms should only be setup on one machine, which typically should be the server (machine that contains the database).

To run e-Comms from the main menu of Bookscan, simply click e-Comms-->e-Comms. To run it..
If this is the first time running it, you will be prompted with a few error messages, dont be alarmed by these are these are just to let you know that e-Comms is not yet setup to run, click OK to both of these messages. If you dont get these prompts, proceed anyway.


1.	From the main menu of e-Comms, click File-->Setup.
2.	On the General Tab, set the Bookscan Path as your database directory (e.g. C:\e-Bilitydata\ ). Set Transfer Interval to appear like: 00:30:00 (HH:MM:SS; this will send/receive every 30 minutes).  Tick the Activity Log box.  No other options should be ticked on this window.   Optionally, if you want e-Comms to start minimised in the System Tray, tick the box labelled Start in Task Bar.
3.  Click on the Connection tab. Set SMTP Server and POP3 Server to your ISP's mail servers, (eg. mail.bigpond.com for Telstra / Bigpond.)
4.  Click on the EDI tab. Fill in your  username (Note: you need to leave the pac/p part of the username, followed by your SAN number. e.g. pac/p123456 ) and password (obtain from ECN group).   The FTP Server field will be prefilled with ftp.pacstream.com.au.
5.  Enter you normal email address in the Store EDI Email Address field.
5.  Click Ok button to save the settings.

This will now have you set up to run e-Comms to facilitate any orders that will be made, and/or electronic invoices ready to be received from suppliers.

Suggestion: If you wish to have e-Comms automatically run when your machine starts (it will start minimised in the bottom right hand corner of windows, next to the clock if you selected this in step 2 above), you will need to add a shortcut. To do this open My Computer. Go to C:\Documents and Settings\ Click on the username folder listed under this that you are logged on as. e.g. 'MainUser' . Then you want to select Start Menu, Programs, then Bookscan. (You will now be in C:\Users\MainUser\AppData\Roaming\Microsoft\Windows\Start Menu\Programs\Bookscan).
Right click on 'e-Comms.exe' and select the option 'Copy'. Now go to C:\Users\MainUser\AppData\Roaming\Microsoft\Windows\Start Menu\Programs\Startup and right click again and select 'Paste'


Electronic Ordering of Stock

To create orders and send them through Pacstream, click here to see how to order electronically.

Note: For orders to send, e-Comms must be running and either setup to run automatically or triggered manually, otherwise they wont be sent through to the suppliers.

Electronic Invoice receiving

Electronic Invoices section are for stock coming in from suppliers. The supplier need to be setup to send them out (not all do) and they need to know that you accept them.

When e-Comms is running to send electronic orders it will at the same time download electronic invoices that are waiting to be picked up. To ensure you are setup, ensure you have followed steps 1 to 3 above.

To process electronic orders that are downloaded from Pacstream, click here to see what you need to do.

.topic 2410

Typically when there is a new version, or any file for that matter, (these steps are not file specific unless otherwise instructed by support), we will either email you a link to the new version or you will view it on our website (http://support.barcode.com.au Log in with your email address & password, then go to the Software Downloads page to download), to then be installed.
On the website, click the link of the file you wish to download.
	OR
If emailed as shown below, click on the link(s) also.




It will then display the above (through our website, or a link emailed), click the Save button.


Browse for where we wish to put it; what want to save it in the e-Bilitydownloads folder that was setup in the pre-install instructions. (You could chose to save it somewhere else, such as a USB thumbdrive.)  Select L:\e-Bilitydownloads\ as shown above.


Click the Save button.


It will then show this window, which will start downloading the file to the specified location. When done, the window will close automatically (to stop it automatically closing, uncheck 'Close this dialog box when download completes'. Doing it like this will let you know that it downloaded completely).

You can then go into Computer then navigate to where you saved the files to access them.
