.topic 2140

Prerequisites
·	Internet access at each location. Either by dial-up or direct connection via ADSL, Cable, Wide-Area Network, etc.
·	A dedicated email address for each site location. These email addresses will be used purely for the transfer of multi-store data and must be separate from the email addresses used for email correspondence.
·	Made up codes (typically non-book product) have to standardized across all locations.
·	2 character store (or location) codes allocated to each store and defined at all locations. This is important for the Bookscan configuration steps 2 and 5.
Central Accounts and Central Rewards. All locations, Head Office and Branches, will require a common Customer file to run Central Accounts. For stores starting from scratch, this is implemented seamlessly. For stores already running separate Customer databases, steps will have to be taken to unify a common Customer file across all locations. Once implemented, this will update as new customers are added.


Configuration
From the main menu of Bookscan click on e_Comms.

(If you get the error please edit the transfer setup information or  data path is invalid, just click on OK.)
The following will be displayed.



Setup

 When in e-Comms to run setup on the main menu click File then click Setup;
This screen is split into 5 tabs as shown in the images below.
	

General tab

The Bookscan path must be set to the location of your data files.
The Transfer Interval is displayed in the form hh:mm:ss and will determine how long e-Comms waits after the last completed transfer before running again.  Set it to 00:00:00 if you want to run on demand.
Disabling Transfers Incoming will allow the e-Comms transfer to send data only if it will not try to pick up any detail from the email account.
Disabling Transfers Outgoing will stop any email being sent and will allow Bookscan to pick up details only.
The Activity Log, if checked, will record the activity log to a text file. The text file, called actlog.txt, is located in the Logs folder in the database shown in Bookscan Path.
To view the log file, click the View Log File button.  (This may take a long time to open if the log file is very large.)
 Pressing Start in Task Bar will automatically minimise e-Comms to the taskbar when it is started.




Connection tab

The Dial Up Networking Entry (obsolete & ghosted)
The Dial Up user name (obsolete & ghosted)
The Dial Up password (obsolete & ghosted)
The SMTP Server is the SMTP server name of your ISP (Defaults to mail.bigpond.com)
The POP3 Server is POP3 server name of your ISP (Defaults to mail.bigpond.com)
The Direct Connection (obsolete & ghosted)



Web Orders tab
This tab is only to be used for stores wishing to transfer between sites using the email method (old). The new transmission method FTP and Local network is contained on the EDI tab. Please refer to that for it setup. If using FTP/Local network options, ensure this tab's fields (Multi Store) stays blank.

Account: Email address used for web site registration & admin login.
Password: Web site admin password
Web URL: web site sub-domain. (The first part of the whole address.  Leave out the e-web.com.au)
e-Web Site ID: This can be copied from the website admin via Site Settings / API integration
Jacket FTP User ID: Obsolete, leave empty.
Jacket FTP password: Obsolete, leave empty.
Check for web orders: Optional, used to have e-Comms automatically check for & download web orders.




Sales Export tab
Used if you are sending data to Nielsen Book Scan.

Nielsen Bookscan: Export Settings
FTP Server is the FTP internet address where you will be connecting to send the Bookscan data.
Username is the account ID that has been supplied from Nielsen Bookscan.
The Password is for the Username, also supplied from Nielsen Bookscan.
Enable Nielsen Bookscan is to be ticked if you wish to enable this to be used.

Master onhand export: settings
FTP / None: Select FTP if you wish to export your onhand figures to an FTP server.
FTP Server:  FTP server address
Username:  FTP server username
Password:  FTP server password


E.D.I. tab
Interstore Communication via:
This is for businesses with multiple sites wishing to communicate with each other (sending stock transfers, product information etc). There are the 3 methods that can be selected:
Local network : Type in Folder Path or browse (yellow icon) to select the shared folder that all sites can see. This folder location is where both sites will be placing and retrieving their files from.
FTP : When selected, then fill in the FTP server address, Username and Password for access to that server. The FTP server must be the same for all sites.
None: No interstore communication.


Note: When using FTP and local network method you need to make sure you have full read/write access to these locations.
Note: When FTP and local network methods run the first time, they create folders in these locations for each of the stores, using their short store code as the folders name.



Pacstream FTP Settings:
FTP settings are used for sending Pacstream data
FTP Server is the address of the Pacstream FTP server
Username is the username supplier by Pacstream
Password is the password supplied by Pacstream.
Store EDI Email Address is the email address that all email will display in the From field, also see section on configuring Customers and Suppliers for e-mail communications.
It is also the email that will be replied to by Pacstream.




eWeb tab
This information is for usage of those who have Barcode Solutions: php  selected in System Options / Modules tab.
(Obsolete export sysem, no longer supported.)

FTP Server is the address of the FTP server where your website is being hosted.
Username is the username for connecting to your FTP server.
Password is the password for your FTP account.
Export Directory is the location on the FTP server that the files need to be saved to. This is where the website will load files from.
Image Directory is the location on the FTP server that the images need to be saved to. This is where the website will load images from.
Web Encryption Key is for if your website has uses encryption and requires a key.





When all the tabs have been completed click OK button.

On the main e-Comms screen click File then click Transfer.
Now click Start button , to start sending data.
It will now send data all requested data to the required locations including: -

·	Email and EDI transaction advices to Customers and Suppliers.
·	Inter-store data.
·	Synchronisation's request resends.
·	Nielsen Bookscan.

If you set a time in the Transfer Interval during configuration, you will see it counting down, (00:29:55 in the image below.).  You can still run it manually by clicking Start, if you so choose.
 See below,


2. Synchronise
Click on File then click Synchronise.
Here you can synchronise Titles Customers, Stock level, Title Categories, and Customer Categories between participating stores.
Each store must do this to ensure the databases are in unison

See below,

3. Request Resend
Click on File then click Request Resend.
The request resend function allows you request information from a particular store.

See below,


·	Stock transfers
      If you select Stock Transfer you a have to enter the Transfer Number you are requesting.
      At the bottom of the window you will need to fill in the Branch Code field for the store you are transferring to.

·    Store Sales History
      This will send the store sales history to National office (Paper Plus only)

·    Store Sale transactions
      This will send a consolidated sales history for use at National office (Paper Plus only)

·     History and transactions
       A combination of the above (Paper Plus only)




.topic 2160

Problem:
When installing on a Window NT workstation you will get the following error.
The application or DLL *:\plusscan\bsa\bs5\disk1\cain4shll.dll is not a valid Windows NT image
Or
Unable to load cain4shl.dll. Desktop objects and icons may not be set up.
Please check against your installation diskette
Answer:
Ignore the error and continue with the installation. E-COMMS will install successfully.


Problem:
Dynamic memory low error.
Answer:
This is caused when a large file is in the mailbox. Remove the large file and try again.


Problem:
e-Comms caused an invalid page fault in module cavoole.dll
Answer:
This is caused when a large file is in the mailbox. Remove the large file and try again.


Problem:
Unable to send transfers time out error.
Answer:
Go to e-Comms \ File \ Setup and disable incoming
Try again.
Go to e-Comms \ File \ Setup and disable outgoing
Doing this will give you and indication of where the problem lies.
It will also prove that e-Comms communications works and the problem is with a file.

You can also rename the <database folder>\transfer directory to Transfer.old and then start e-Comms while you are on line. OK the error port already in use and e-Comms will automatically set up the transfer directory again.
Get the customer to dial again and e-Comms will now connect.
Or
Make sure the SMTP and POP3 settings are correct


Problem:
e-Comms does not appear to start after installation.
Answer:
Another copy of e-Comms is already running, check the task bar.  If not found, go to Start - Run.  Type in Taskmgr & click OK.  Go to the Processes tab & look for e-comms.exe.  If it is there, select it & click End Process.

.topic 143
Command Window

Command Windows can be opened from the Report Writer's File menu (first selection) or by clicking on the leftmost toolbar button.  The Command Window gives you access to all reports, as well as means of organising its list.
It opens by default when you open the report Writer from the Bookscan menu Reports / Reports.



The left-most pane of the window contains an entry for each report in the system (these can be system reports or ones that you have created yourself: custom reports).  The right half of the window contains a number of displays and buttons.  The upmost control contains Notes concerning the report.  You can edit these to your liking.   Beneath the Notes display there can appear a record of how long the report last took to run (not shown).  Beneath that is a checkbox by which previewing can be switched on or off.  The next checkbox allows deleted reports to be viewed in the list (allowing them to be undeleted).  And beneath that come the push-buttons, described below:

Run  		-  Runs the selected report.
More Info  	-  Shows the Help on the selected report.
Rename  	-  Allows you to rename the report.
Modify  		-  Loads the report form into the ReportPro Designer.
Up In List  	-  Moves the report up in the master list.
Down In List  	-  Moves the report down in the master list.
Create  		-  Allows you to add a new report to the master list.
Remove  	-  Remove the report from the master list, or recall deleted reports.
Close		-  Closes the command window.

.topic 126
Executing Report Writer From The Command Line

Certain reports can be obtained from Report Writer programatically (e.g.: from a batch-file).   This enables Bookscan users to take "single-click" advantage of Report Writer's superior report-presentation whilst continuing to enjoy the benefits of a POS system running under Windows.  The command-line structure uses the following conventions:

   < x:PathToProgram >\Report Writer.EXE   \<ReportIdentifier>  \<Parameter1>  \<Parameter2>  \<Etc.>

Omit the angled brackets from the actual command line.  And only supply the parameters that a particular report (see links below) requires.   Values within the angled brackets are place-holders for  values which must be specified in order to run any particular report.   The reports able to be run by these means include:	Reprint  Invoices,	Transfer Reprint,	Special Orders By Customer,	Special Order (Particular),     Purchase Order Number.


.topic 119
Customer Account Utilities

Customer account terms
To globally change the customer terms in the customer master, go to the Customer account terms area:
Select the "from" terms e.g. 14 days.
Select the "to" terms e.g. 30 days.
Click on the Update Terms button. In the above example all accounts with terms of 14 days will be updated to 30 days.

Customer account type change
If you are changing accounts from open item to balance brought forward,  or the other way around, and there are outstanding transactions on the accounts, then you will need to run this routine to update the existing account transactions.

WARNING: These routines are not completely reversible -  so if you change your mind you may have lost some of your data.  Please contact Barcode Solutions before proceeding. You should also backup your files.
WARNING: Do not change the account type on the customer master. This routine will reverse whatever the account type setting is there.

In the Customer account type area, use the radio buttons to select whether to globally change all accounts or to just change a single customer.
In the case of a single customer, select the customer from the combo box.
Click on the Update Account button.

Effects of this routine:
Open item to balance brought forward:
The customer account type will be changed to balance brought forward.
Transactions older than 30 days will be moved to the history file. In debtors will only see the current transactions.

Balance brought forward to open item:
The customer account type will be changed to open item.
Transactions older than 30 days will be totaled for each of 30, 60 and 90 days and a  journal added to the account for each of these (where they are non-zero). You will now be able to allocate payments and credits against these debit journals.

Customer Account Recovery
If the customer account type has been changed and there were outstanding transactions on the account and the above routine was not run, then running month end will cause the account to display anomalous figures. This routine will repair those figures and re-calculate the current, 30, 60 and 90 day figures. If figures are correct then the routine will have no effect.
Backup of your files.
In the Customer Account Correction area, just click on the Recovery button.
When asked if you wish to proceed, click Yes button.
If there are any discrepancies for any customers a window will display with the current, 30, 60 and 90 day figures and the correct figures for each in error only.
To fix, click Repair button

 Supplier Account Updates

Supplier account type change
If you are changing supplier accounts from open item to balance brought forward,  or the other way around, and there are outstanding transactions on the accounts, then you will need to run this routine to update the existing account transactions.

WARNING: These routines are not completely reversible -  so if you change your mind you may have lost some of your data.  Please contact Barcode Solutions before proceeding. You should also backup your files.
WARNING: Do not change the account type on the supplier master. This routine will reverse whatever the account type setting is there.
setting is in the supplier master.

In the Supplier account type area, use the radio buttons to select whether to globally change all accounts or to just change a single supplier.
In the case of a single supplier, select the supplier from the combo box.
Click on the Update Account button.

Effects of this routine:
Open item to balance brought forward:
The supplier account type will be changed to balance brought forward.
Transactions older than 30 days will be moved to the history file. In creditors will only see the current transactions.

Balance brought forward to open item:
The supplier account type will be changed to open item.
Transactions older than 30 days will be totaled for each of 30, 60 and 90 days and a  journal added to the account for each of these (where they are non-zero). You will now be able to allocate payments and credits against these debit journals.

Supplier recovery
The supplier recovery repairs open item supplier accounts that have errors in them. If figures are correct then the routine will have no effect.
Backup of your files.
In the Supplier Account Correction area, click on the Recovery button.
When asked if you wish to proceed, click Yes button.
If there are any discrepancies for any supplier, a window will display with the current, 30, 60 and 90 day figures and the correct figures for each in error.
To fix, click Repair button


.topic 201
This feature of Invoice Entry enables you to quickly invoice all special orders for all customers where stock has been received.
The process loads in turn, into the header, each customer who has special orders where the stock has been received.
The special order lines for the first customer are automatically copied into the browser. You can then finalise the order in the usual way by clicking on the Finalise button. The invoice will then be created and the next customer and their special orders are loaded. Use the Skip button to skip processing any customer.

Processing Details
Header window
·	In the Invoice Entry window ensure that you have not selected a customer and that the Transaction Type is set to Standard Invoice.
·	Click on the Start Batch Alt F8 button to begin processing.
·	The number of customers to be processed and the total number of special order lines will be displayed.
·	The first customer with special orders will be loaded.
·	Make any changes you require in the header (e.g. re-set the terms ) and click on the Save F12 button.

Browser Window
·	The browser showing that customer's special orders will now be displayed.
·	Amend or delete any lines you need to and add further lines to the invoice if required.
·	Click on the Finalise F12 button.

Footer window
·	Make any changes or additions you require to the footer window (e.g. add a freight charge) and click on the Post F12 button to create the invoice.
·	The next customer will now be loaded and you can process their orders in the same way.
·	To skip a customer click on the Skip Customer Alt F9 button; the next customer will be loaded in its place.
·	Add any time you can use the End Batch F8 button to stop processing.
·	When the last customer with available special orders is finished, batch mode will end automatically.



.topic 175
Handling small orders
The cost of freight and invoice handling can make small orders uneconomic. To overcome this, Bookscan offers the following features to allow users to trap small orders and hold them until further orders have been received. As further orders are received from a customer, or as their backorders are released, the system checks the total value of the customer's order and when the values moves above the minimum, removes them from being on hold, allowing them to be processed.
Orders held this way can be monitored and controlled in the Picking window and can be manually taken off hold and if required, either a surcharge or a freight charge added.

System Options
On the Sales tab there is a Surcharge As combo box with three choices:
1.	None
Set this option if you wish not to use the minimum order features.
2.	Small order surcharge
Select this option to hold  orders that are below the minimum value or to apply a small order surcharge if they are to be released. If using this option, enter the Small Order Surcharge amount and Minimum Order Value in the fields below the combo box. For example you may set a standard surcharge of $7.00 on all orders below $60.00.
3.	Hold order below minimum
Select this option to hold  orders below the minimum value but not use the small order surcharge features. If using this option, enter Minimum Order Value in the field below the combo box.
Also on this tab the Consolidate Pick Slips checkbox should be checked to allow the automatic linking of customers' picking slips..

Invoice Entry
The following features will only apply if you have selected either option 2 or 3 above.

Current order summary
After you have saved the header information and ISBN/Catalogue field becomes visible, a summary of the customers current orders is shown in the In Picking box. The information shown is for all this customer's unprinted orders for that are waiting to be processed. This items shown are:
	In picking	The value of all unprinted picking slips for this customer.
	Freight total	The total value of the freight charged on these pick slips.
	Surcharges	The total value of surcharges on these pick slips.
	Backorders	The total value of backorders for this customer.
This allows the operator to make decisions on charging freight or adding a surcharge.

Surcharges
Option 2
If you have selected option 2 for small order surcharges, you can add the surcharge by clicking on the Surcharge button. This is a toggle button. Clicking the button the:
	1st time	Adds the small order surcharge to Surcharge field
	2nd time	Sets the Surcharge field to zero and marks the order as Waive surcharge.
	3rd time	Removes the Waive surcharge Flag.
Orders flagged as waive surcharge will not be held if they are below the minimum.

Option 3
If you have chosen option 3 (hold orders below minimum), you can use this button to mark/unmark the order as Not to be held. Again orders that as so marked are not be held if they are below the minimum order value.

Posting orders
When posting orders the system takes into account the customer's current orders that are waiting to be processed when checking for orders below the minimum order value. If the value of the current order plus the customer's orders already in the system is below the minimum, a warning will be given to the operator that the order will be held, allowing the operator the option to return to the order entry screen to edit the order or to continue with posting the order.
If the current order brings the total value of orders for this customer above the minimum then the held flag will be automatically removed from all their current orders allowing the picking slips to be printed  and the orders despatched. This is the principal advantage of this system - that releasing the orders is fully automated.
If the current order brings the total value of orders for this customer above the minimum and a surcharge has already been applied to any of the orders, the operator will be given the option to have the surcharges removed automatically (option 2 only.)

If the Consolidate Pick Slips option has been selected, then when posting,  the current order will be automatically linked with the customer's orders already in the system.

Picking Window
The following features will only apply if you have selected either option 2 or 3 above.
There is now a Held column in the Picking window. This is checked for orders which are flagged as held because they are below the minimum order level. Picking slips flagged as held cannot be printed.

Taking orders off hold
Held orders are automatically taken off hold when other picking slips for customer are created, or backorders are released,  to bring the total value of the orders above the minimum order value.  To take an order off  hold manually, double click on the checkbox in the Hold column. If you have selected option 2 (for small order surcharges) you will be given the option of adding the small order surcharge. If you have selected option 2 and no freight has been charged, you will be given the option of adding a freight charge.

Held Lines
There is now a button to show only held orders. This is a toggle button; clicking it the:
	1st time		Shows only held orders.
	2nd time		Shows all lines except held lines.
	3rd time		Shows both held and unheld lines.

Display mailorder customers
You can now display only mailorder customers. Right click in the browser to display the context menu and select one of the three options:
	Mailorder only	Shows only mailorder customers.
	Exclude mailorder	Shows only debtor customers	
	Show all		Shows both mailorder and debtor customers
Mailorder customers are now shown with a yellow  background in the browser.

Improved linking
When authorising a payment in the picking window or applying a payment for a mailorder customer, the order will now automatically be linked the customer's other orders. At the same time a check is done to see whether any Held flags can be removed.


Backorder Release
New window
There is a new window for backorder releases with the following features:
It has a browser for displaying backorders. There are three methods of filtering the browser display:
·	All backorders
· 	By customer – display only backorders for the customer selected in the Customer field.
·	By product – display only backorders for the product selected in the Product field.

Sorting the browser
Where all backorders are displayed, you can change the sort order by clicking on the appropriate column header.
	Pick # or date column		Sort chronologically
	Cust key or customer		Sort in customer key order
	Product code or Title		Sort by product code

Releasing backorders
Backorders are released by clicking on the Release (F12) button. Backorders will only be released for those lines displayed in the browser.
·	So if you have displayed backorders only for a particular customer, then only that customer's backorders will be released.
·	Similarly if you are showing only backorders for a certain product then clicking on the Release button will release backorders only for that product.
· 	If All Backorders is selected then a full backorder release will be done.

Holding backorders
To prevent a backorder from releasing, you can set it to held by double clicking its checkbox in the Held column. To remove the flag double click on the check box again.
To hold all displayed lines, right click to display the context menu and select Hold All.
To remove the Held flag on all lines in the browser, click on the Remove Held button.
To remove the Held flag on all lines, not just those in the browser, check the Remove all Held checkbox and then click on the Remove Held button.
An example: you want to release a single line for a customer.
	Move the cursor to the Customer field and enter the customer key.
	Right click on the browser and select Hold All
	Double click on the Held checkbox of the line you want to release
	Click on the Release (F12)  button

Linking
If the Consolidate Pick Slips option in System Options has been selected, released backorders will be automatically  linked to any unprinted picking slips for that customer.
If the total value of these orders is below the minimum order value, the released order will be flagged as "Held."
If the released order brings the total value of the customer's current orders to above the minimum, then all the held flags will be automatically removed and the customers orders will be available for despatch..

Backorder reports
There are now three backorder reports that can be run from this window and from Bookscan Reports. These are:
	All Backorders ( sorted by either date, customer or product code).
	For a single customer.
	For a single product code.
To run the reports, select either the customer or the product to display them in the browser and then click on the Print (F11) button.
For all backorders, select the All Backorders option. If required change the sort order by clicking on the appropriate column header ( as outlined above). Then click on the Print (F11) button.

Handling backorders where there is insufficient stock
Where there is insufficient stock to cover all backorders for a certain product, you can limit the maximum quantity to be allocated to each order.
Select the product in the product field and then enter the maximum quantity you want allocated any order. Then click on the Release (F12) button.

.topic 21
From within the report designer a preview of the report can be obtained from the File menu.  If the report contains variables whose values are reset when the report-proper is run, you can emulate the consequences simply by editing the default value stored in the report form.  This should not have any effect on runtime output.

Caution: 	 If you modify the default of a  variable which is 0 (zero) then you may upset totalling calculations.



.topic 174

Pressing the Recall Parked button will bring up the following dialog.



This dialog will list the available invoices for each supplier that have been parked. Highlight the invoice you want to recall and press
the Select button to load the parked receiving session back into the receiving window. Now you can continue were you left off from.
If you want to start again you can delete a parked session using the Delete button.
If there are a built up amount of parked sessions that are all, you can click on the 'Delete All' button, which will clear all lines.
When you start receiving if you enter a supplier and invoice number that have been parked then this session will
automatically load into the receiving window, without having to press thee Recall Parked button (From the main receiving window)

.topic 70
Selection Controls

In choosing the parameters for a report you will be using fairly standard Windows controls  -  checkboxes, radio-buttons, combo-lists and edit-lines.   A couple of spare observations may be of benefit...

Where lists are presented in an open (rather than a drop-down / combo) fashion you can usually make multiple selections, simply by mouse-clicking on multiple items.  Click a second time to deselect an item.  A button captioned "Select All" accompanies each multiple selection list and performs as you'd expect.  If you use it, then the functional caption changes to "Deselect All",  minimising your need to issue instructions.

Where date-input is availed, the edit control is post fixed by a button which opens a calendar control (wherefrom the full selection can be made by double-clicking on a date).   Various means of navigation are provided within the calendar control.  In the edit control, one component of the date is highlighted at a time.  The plus and minus keys increment/decrement the selected component.  The arrow keys select the next component (day, month or year).  Experiment with all of the selection controls.  They're the bulk of the interface!

Some of the edit-controls accept a partial value (e.g.: a partial title for the Titles On Hold report) and Report Writer will retrieve the remainder of the information as part of validating the parameters (in this case you'll need to click the OK button a second time in order to launch the report).  Report Writer generally performs validation as soon as you click the OK button (to run the report).  Read warning messages fully and you won't ever get stuck for long!

.topic 166
Standard Variables

Report Writer often sets a value (that is stored within the report form) at runtime.  The name of the variable can have been anything, and "ReportTitle" is one that we have often used.  By using it, we enabled the report form to produce a variable "title", depending on settings selected for the report.

The "standard variables" are sent to all reports, even though some forms don't use them.  But you can incorporate them into any report by creating a variable of the designated name within the form (by modifying the report


The standard variables which you can employ in this manner are:

NAME			PURPOSE

"Licensee"  		-	to include your company's registered name.
"TaxName"		-	the name of the sales tax you endure (e.g.: GST).
"GSTRate"		-	the tax rate set for your installation.
"CurrencySign"	-	your system's currency symbol (e.g.: $).
"ISBN_DESC"	-	what your system uses the ISBN field for (typically: ISBN).
"AUTH_DESC"	-	what "author" stands for on your system (e.g.: is "Artist" on DiscScan systems).
"IMPR_DESC"	-	how your system uses the Imprint field (e.g.: "Label" on DiscScan systems).

Note: These values are not set when you are modifying a report.  Default values are used in that case.

.topic 1345

This window allows you to set the number of copies you will print of each document and set some other system defaults, as listed below.


Special orders
Set the number of copies that will print for each of the following reports:
·	Deletion advice: The docket printed when a special order is cancelled.
·	Advice notes: The slips with customer special order details printed during receiving.
·	Customer letters: If the customer master has the Special Order Letters box checked, letters to the customer (instead of Advice Notes) will be produced during the receiving of special orders.
·	Dockets: The number of dockets to print out when the special order is taken.

Other settings are:
·	Group by customer: When ticked, the setting causes advice notes and customer letters to print in customer. This is useful if you are posting them as the multiple slips for a single customer can be easily collated to go in the same envelope. When unticked, the slips will be grouped by item.  This makes it easier to match the slips with titles.
·	Special labels: Special order letters and slips print when the good received note is printed during the receiving process. Selecting this option will also cause the program also to print a (barcode-type) label with the customers name and phone number to stick on arriving special order stock.
·	Min stock to zero: This will set the minimum stock level to zero after a special order has been received. Use this if you want to ensure that no special ordered items go into draft purchase orders.

Invoicing
Her you can set the number of copies of invoices and credit notes to print from the Order Entry window.

Other settings are:
·	Default sort order:  Determines the order which titles are display on the invoices and credit notes.
·	Auto backorder: When checked, the system automatically generates a special order for a customer when there is insufficient stock to fill a line on an invoice.

Laybys
Set the number of copies that will print for layby dockets, layby deletion advice dockets and layby payment receipts.

Other settings are:
Days to complete: Set the number of days you allow for a layby to be completed. If you have layby condition printing on your docket, the time allowed will be included.

Other forms
Set the number of copies that will print for:
Returns Authorisations.
Debtor payments	
Purchase orders
Credit Claims
EFTPOS dockets
Items on hold: Set the number of dockets to print for items on hold.

.topic 1344

IIn these windows you can set up the Bookscan modules for which you have licenses.
Contact Barcode Solutions to arrange to purchase any extra modules.


Note that the checkboxes for the modules (such as Creditors, Debtors or Marketing) display which modules are licensed. You cannot turn on or off a module in these windows. The checkboxes and other fields for the various module options can be edited.

Accounts modules
There are two types of accounts supported by Bookscan for both creditors and debtors. They are:
·	Open item accounting
Open item accounting retains all transactions on the account until they are paid. At the next ageing, they are then moved to the history file. Payments are applied against specific invoices. Unpaid or part paid items remain on the account until cleared.

·	Balance brought forward
Balance brought forward accounts show only the account balance for anything older than the current month's transactions.
The complete history can be obtained only from previous statements.
Barcode Solutions strongly recommend the use of Open Item accounting as this retains the details of each transaction until cleared.

Creditors options
Balance brought forward credit: Here you can select the default account type that will be displayed when adding a new supplier to the supplier master.
Auto post GRN: With this option selected, supplier receipts are added to the creditor file when posting into stock. If not set, supplier invoices will need to be manually added in the Creditors window.

Debtors options
Open items debtors: See the Balance BF credit in Creditors above.

Account Export
This module allows you to export all or part of your Bookscan accounts directly into MYOB.

Web Site (Please contact Barcode Solutions for further information.)
Web:  Select the typre of web site you are using.
e-Web Drop Ship Allowed: Allows the e-Web order to be drop shipped.
Path for Bookscan Large images: Set the path to the location where large images are stored.
WINSCP session name:  The name of the WINSCP session created for exporting images to the web site.
Generate WINSCP script:  Generates a script to call WINSCP to export the images.  Can be used to run the image export as a scheduled task in Windows.

Marketing
This module allows you to report your sales against your promotional material (source) and/or against where your customers live (location).
To turn off reporting by source, tick the Disable Source checkbox. To turn off reporting by location, tick the Disable Location checkbox. If you wish, use the combo boxes to set defaults.

Other options
·	Multiple types per sale: Check this box if you wish to know the location and the source for each item, not each transaction.
·	Force Selection: Check this box if you wish to enforce the selection of the marketing options (by default in cash sales and invoicing, you can click 'Cancel' when it prompts for a selection).

Other modules
Distributor/publisher: This option gives users access to the warehousing functions. Picking slips can be created in the Order Entry window. There are special windows to control your picking operations, a back order control window and a scan pack despatch window for packing your orders. There is also an automated returns authorisation area to control customer return requests. The returns policy for this module is set on the Options tab window.
Promotions:  This module allows you to set special pricing for selected products for a period of time.
Academic: Assists in the purchasing for books for universities and colleges.
Consignments inwards: Allows you receive products on consignment from suppliers. The module fully automates the tracking of you consignment sales and allows you to report sales, returns, shrinkage etc back to you suppliers.
Consignments out: Allows you to consign stock to your customers. It operates in a similar way to approvals but has more invoicing and control options.
Security: This option allows you to restrict user access to the various parts of Bookscan.



Distributor/publisher:
Returns policy:
You can set up your customer returns policy here. You allow some items to be sold as "sale or return" and others with stricter return conditions (or no returns).
You can also set a default expiry time for returns authorisations.

Other settings
Coles Myer: Bookscan is Coles/Myer electronic delivery compliant. If you wish to set this up please contact Barcode solutions.
Don't use picking slips: Using the checkbox, you can choose not to use the picking and packing features and just invoice your customers.
Bin locations: This option allows you to auto-allocate warehouse bin locations as new products are received. It also enables the warehouse mapping functionality.

Rewards cards
Rewards cards defaults can be set up here. See the rewards card section for details.

Pre-pack
The Pre-pack module is used to assist in selling to primary and secondary schools. Contact Barcode Solutions if you are interested in this module.

 	         Return to System Options Summary

.topic 1343

Here you can set the defaults for the purchasing screens.


·	Purchase order number sequences: Displays the last-used purchase order number for each of the PO sequences. You can add a sequence or edit the sequence names. You can have up to 5 order number sequences.
WARNING: Please ensure that the sequences do not overlap.

·	Never print barcodes: Turn off barcode printing.	

·	Always print barcodes: When posting your received stock, always print a barcode label.

·	Use catalogue number for ordering: With this flag set, the catalogue number, where available, will be used in place of ISBNs on purchase orders and in the purchasing windows.

·	Don't use minimum stock for reordering: This flag causes the program to use the sales quantity method of re-ordering instead on the minimum stock method. See the Draft PO chapter for details.

·	Stock adjustment supplier: To enable you to view a history of stock adjustments, stock adjustments are posted against a dummy supplier. Create the stock adjustment supplier (usually supplier code STK, supplier name Stock Adjustment) in the Supplier Master. Then select this supplier in the combo box here.

·	Order quantity depends on: Select whether the program uses the on hand or the available stock level when decided when to add a product to draft purchase.

·	Sort draft PO by: Here you can set the default sort products in the draft purchase orders browser by either author or title.

·	Copies to print: You can set the number of copies to be printed for finalised purchase orders and credit claims.

·	Show special comments in editing draft PO:  Display the special order comments in the draft purchase order screen.

·	Preview report of finalised purchase orders: Sets by-passing the print preview as the default when finalising purchase orders.

Auto-hold all draft orders: Put new draft orders on hold

.topic 1341
This window has various system options which you can set.

 	

First column
·	Hold special orders:  If you tick this checkbox, all new special orders are automatically put On Hold. So if you have 4 copies of an item and 3 are for special orders, the on-hand quantity will be 4 and the quantity available will be 1.This will affect when products appear on the purchase order draft. If your re-ordering is dependent on quantity available (set on the Purchasing tab), this box should be checked. If it depends on the on-hand quantity, leave it unchecked.

·	Always pop up Holds: Checking this box will cause the Holds window to always pop up whenever a copy of a title that has holds on it, is scanned into a sale. (Normally it will pop up when there is insufficient stock to cover the sale and the stock on hold).

·	Credit note to backorder: When you check this box credit notes are added to the incoming list. This allows special orders to be allocated against the returned stock.

(If using this option you will need to set up a supplier with supplier code "!C/N").

·	Redirect customer billing: This flag allows customers to be set up with sub-accounts.

·	Allow cash out at point of sale: If you allow EFTPOS cash out at your registers, tick this box.

·	Split register takings: If you check this box the X Report run at end of day will subtotal for each register. This allows for separate balancing of cash at each terminal.

·	Sales against kit components: When selling kits, the sale can be recorded either against the kit or against the kit's component products.
·	Prompt for operator initials: When logging in, prompt the operator for their initials and password.

·	Net Price Override Flag: When the Allow Invoice Net Price Override box is ticked, users in invoice entry can change the price of or add a line discount to net priced items. The items will still be unaffected by header discounts. The option is useful for wholesale/retail operations where you want to prevent discounts on retail sales via cash sales but invoice at a trade price as a wholesaler.

·	Instant Rewards: Tick the box to use rewards at the point of sales.  Select from the list of rewards programs for the program you wish to use.  In the example above, the "Valued Customer" program has been selected.

·	Always pop up Ins.Rew.: Will cause a pop up window to appear in the cash sales window notifying of the current reward "points" or the dollar value the customer can use if they have passed the threshold set in the reward program.

Second column
·	Don't track categories of purchases: Turns off the tracking of customer purchases by category.

·	Retain invoice comments as default: When you check this box if you enter a comment when creating an invoice, that comment will appear as the default on subsequent invoices created in that session.

·	Default to singles in POS: When you tick this box, the default entry in the POS window will be for sales of a single quantity per line.

·	No discount if below margin: This flag will stop discounting in the order entry window and in the POS where the margin on a sale line is below the minimum margin set in the next field.

·	Minimum invoice sales margin: Set a minimum invoice margin. In invoice entry, where a title's margin is below this minimum, the user will be prompted before saving the line. This is used to prevent incorrectly priced items being invoiced.

·	Surcharge as: Select the small order option you wish to enable- none, hold order below a minimum value or small order surcharge.

·	Minimum purchase value: Set a minimum value for invoices to avoid being classed as a small order.

·	Small order surcharge: Set the default small order surcharge amount for invoice orders under the minimum purchase value.

·	Payments discretionary adjustment: This field is useful for invoicing of mail orders, where customers send in payments with their orders. If the payment amount is slightly below the actual invoice total, you may wish to accept the payment amount as full payment. Here you can set a default amount for an acceptable payment difference (e.g. 50 cents) at which the system will process a short payment as full payment.

Third column
·	Consolidate daily sales: If you are using department groups, checking this box will give group subtotals on the Daily Sales Report.
Departments in a group all have same initial letter in their department code.
E.g. you might have eight book departments starting with B (BCH, BHE etc) and three stationery departments that start with S (SGE etc). With this box checked, The Daily Sales Report will show subtotals on Books and Stationery.

·	Consolidate picking slips: To enable the consolidating of multiple picking slips onto the one Invoice. This is for Warehousing users.

·	Invoice on docket: Causes invoices created in the POS window to be printed on the docket printer rather than the standard printer.

·	Split store taking: The cash book report will group sales by store areas. The store area is defined by the first letter of each register's name. For example if you have five registers in two areas you could set the registers as Kid1, Kid2, Kid3, Adult1, and Adult2. The cash book would split taking in two groups – 'K" and "A".

·	Enable handling charges: This setting can be used for library suppliers who want to charge customers handling on each order line. Setting this flag will enable the Handling filed in the Order Entry window.

·	Round down cents: Normally over the counter sales totals are rounded to the nearest five or ten cents. Check this box to always round totals down. This field also affects the calculator. With this box checked, it will paste rounded down figures into a field.

·	Round to: Set your country's rounding to 5 or 10 cents.

·	Hold order with backorders: Setting this flag will cause orders in the Order Entry window which are only partially filled to be held until the order is complete.

·	Tender amount exclude backorders: When processing payments in the Order Entry window on order that are only partially filled, you have the choice of taking payment for the whole order including backorders or just that part of the order which can be filled now. The flag sets which of these options will be used as the default in the Tendering window.


	     Return to System Options Summary

.topic 1338

This window allows you to set various system options and allows you to access purchasing budget figures.


Subsidy Module
This module is used for those who have items that can be subsidised, such as government rebate text books, or even used for staff discounts.
This module can only be used if activated by Barcode Solutions.
For information on setting up the Subsidy module and using it, click the link.

Other settings
·	Enquiry key length: This field determines how many characters the title master field is indexed on. The usual number is 25.

·	Lines per page on pre-printed invoice forms: This field determines how many lines will be printed per page on invoice forms.

·	Customer key length: Set the length of the alpha part of the customer key. It is usually set to 5. The maximum is 8.

·	No Auto Numbering for customer key: As customer keys are created, the program adds a number (usually three digits) to the end of the alpha section of key –which is usually the customer's surname. So when creating your 53rd customer called Smith, the customer key becomes SMITH053. Setting this flag turns this auto-numbering off.

·	Shopper key must be unique: When creating a new customer, the shopper key is checked for uniqueness.

·	Bookscan style address: This legacy setting can be ignored by almost all users.  (This legacy setting can be ignored by almost all users.)

·	Browse with lines separating rows: Display lines between rows of the title, customer and supplier masters.

·	Legacy account exports: This legacy setting can be ignored by almost all users.

·	Academic with Semesters: Used by universities that use semesters for their courses.

Budgets
If you are using the purchasing budget option, you can set the budget figures here. Click on the Open to Buy Budgets button. For details click on the link.

	      Return to System Options Summary

.topic 2330




.topic 1342

The docket and label printing options are displayed here. When printing reports and forms in Bookscan, the Windows default printer is used. However for barcode labels, dockets, tickets and carton labels, Bookscan prints direct to the printer, bypassing Windows. On this tab, Bookscan is configured to print to these printers.

Barcode label (L1) & Barcode label (L1)
In these two fields, you can set lines to print out at the bottom of each barcode label. Usually the company name is entered on the first line. Note that there may not be sufficient room for the second line on smaller labels.	

Docket head line 1 & Docket head line 2
2 lines that will print at the top of the docket.  Usually store name and suburb or town.

Docket Greeting
Farewell that is printed on the bottom of the docket.

Print ISBN on docket
The ISBN (code) of items will print on the sales docket underneath the Title.

Don't display customer addresses
This checkbox is used to suppress the printing of customer addresses on sales dockets.

Terms & conditions
Sales
You can create a text file listing your sales terms and conditions e.g. your refund policy. This will print on your cash sales docket just before the docket footer.

Layby
You can also create another text file to replace the default layby conditions with your own conditions. To use this text file in place of the default terms, uncheck the Use Default check box. The text files can be as long as you want.

To create a terms & conditions text file:
·	Select either the Sales Docket or Layby Docket radio button.
·	Click on the Add Line (F6) button. The Line field will be enabled and cursor will move there.
·	Enter the first line of your text file (e.g. REFUND POLICY) and click the Save Line (F6) button.
·	Keep typing and saving each line. Press the Escape (Esc) key when finished.

To edit a file:
·	Click on the line you want to edit. The line will be displayed in the Line field.
·	Click on the Edit Line (F5) button. The Line field will be enabled and cursor will move there.
·	Type you changes and click the Save Line (F6) button.

For layby terms, you can click on the Copy Default (F4) button (only visible if no layby terms have been entered). The default terms will be copied into the text box where you can edit them before saving them as your own terms.

                  Return to System Options Summary

.topic 1339

In this window you can set up the defaults for your Title Master.

·	Compulsory title master fields: Choose fields in the Title Master that are to be compulsory during data entry – imprints, binding, status or category.
·	Minimum stock: Sets the default which will appear during data entry. This is usually set to 1. It is advisable not to set it to 0 as that setting prevents the title appearing in the purchase order draft.
·	Review locked records after: Determines how long (in seconds) a title record can be open in edit mode before you are prompted to release the lock on the title.
·	Default active status: Set one of your statuses to be the standard status for a current product.
·	Available stock definition: Define how you want Available stock to be defined.
·	Costs Price: Select the definition of the cost price that you will use- either latest cost price or average cost price. Stock valuations will not be affected as these report both average and last cost prices.
·	Next dummy ISBN: If you wish to use dummy ISBNs (or EANs) for products without barcodes, you can set the sequence here. For example: if you set the sequence here as 0000254856695, then, when you add a new title you can click the Next (Alt F2) to load the next number in the sequence. The number stored in this window is then incremented to the next valid, unused number.

If you enter an ISBN (i.e. 13 digits starting 978 or 979) then the last (check) digit will be re-calculated to ensure it is a valid ISBN. So you don't need to know the check digit for your dummy ISBN. Just guess it, and let the program calculate it for you. For example if you enter 9780000100000 as your first dummy code the program will save it as 9780000100009, changing the last digit from 0 to 9.
·	Use Illustrator field: Setting this flag will allow you to record the illustrator of a book in the title master.
·	Firm sale: This refers to whether stock is supplied to you as "sale or return" or as a "firm sale". It sets the default which will appear during data entry. Set it to whichever is more common for your stock.
·	Binding groups: This flag is for use primarily by music wholesalers. It allows you to set each binding type as either Books or Music. When creating invoices and pick slips, the program will report a separate line and total quantity for both binding groups. Once the flag is set, open the Maintain Binding window (Tables/Authority Tables/Binding) to set up your groups.
·	Imprint matrix: Setting this flag will allow you to set up a discount percentage by imprint for customer groups. For example, customers in customer group G could be set to receive a 10% discount on all title with an ABC imprint. Once the flag is set, open the Maintain Imprints window (Tables/Imprint Master) to set up your discounts.  Using the imprint matrix precludes you from using the standard pricing matrix.	
·	Web searches: Set up your user names and passwords for downloading titles from web sites. The products supported are TitlePage, Nielsen Bookfind, Bowker Web Services and Bowker Search Link
·	Author/Illustrator in upper case: Forces these fields to upper case.
·	Always lift the re-price flag in receiving: Also on the Title Master is a flag to remove the re-price flag when stock is first received. In the first case outlined above (NYP titles), the re-price flag is removed but on the second (imported titles) it is not removed and the product is re-priced each time it is received into stock. The flag on this window merely sets the default "Lift re-price" flag on the New Title window.
·	Set re-price as default for new titles: In the distributor/publisher module, backordered products can be set to be re-priced as stock is received. A re-price flag is set on these titles in the Title Master. This is used for titles not yet published, so that you can take orders before a retail price has been set and also for imported title where the price to be set will depend on the exchange rate at the time stock is received. This flag sets the default for the Re-price flag in the New Title window.
·	Enable looseleaf in the Kits module: Bookscan can be configured to handle looseleaf products assembled from other products. Please contact Barcode Solutions if you are interested in setting up this feature.
·	No auto-link imprint/supplier: Stopps Bookscan from automatically loadin supplier information when a linked imprint is selected.


	     Return to System Options Summary

.topic 1346

This is where your business' details are set.

These fields will appear on your various forms and reports such as the invoice form.

Notes:
Town/Suburb
The Town/Suburb field should contain your postcode and, if you wish your state.

Address 2
Address 2 can be left blank.

Country
The Country field needs to be filled in the format "Australia", "New Zealand" or "Singapore". The content of this field has implications elsewhere in the program.

ABN (Australian users) or GST No (New Zealand users)
For Australian users this is your Australian Business Number.
For New Zealand users, it is your GST number.

SAN	
If you have a SAN (Standard Address Number) enter it in the SAN field.
Some users have 2 accounts with suppliers ordering on different SAN numbers. If you have a second SAN, enter it in the Alternate SAN fields.
SANs are used to uniquely identify businesses during electronic ordering and invoicing.

Store Code	
For multi-store set ups, use this field for the two character code for this store.
Branch codes are set up in Tables/ Authority Tables/ Branches.
If operating as a single store, you still need a code here.


Set System Options Password (Alt F9) button
System Options contains settings that should only be changed at management level. You can set a password to restrict access to the System Options window by clicking on this button.
If you have a license for the Security module, setting a password here is unnecessary.

Set Stock Adjust Password (Alt F8) button
You can set a password to restrict doing stock adjustments by clicking on this button.
If you have a license for the Security module, setting a password here is unnecessary.

Group store code
The field is for Angus & Robinson franchisees only. They should enter their A&R five digit store code. The code will be used for sending stock availability data to the A&R web site.

Bank Details
These fields are for banking details that will display on Invoices & end of month Statements to debtors so that clients can pay by Direct Deposit to your account.

 	    Return to System Options Summary

.topic 1337

IIf you have multiple stores, you can set up how they will interact here. Before setting up a multi-store network you should consult Barcode Solutions.
In a multi-store system, Bookscan's e-Comms program can be used to transfer stock, customer and title information between stores.


Multi-store setups
Synchronisation options
Share on-hand figures
Tick this box when using e-Comms to transfer stock figures between stores. The stock levels at other stores can be viewed on the Stocks tab of the Title Master.

Share title changes
If you wish to have the different branches share the changing of titles, check this box. As titles are added to the master or edited, e-Comms will update the title masters at the other stores.

Do not share new titles
If you are sharing title changes between branches but do not want new titles added, tick this box.

Transfer vouchers
Ticking this checkbox will allow you to have tracked gift vouchers used at all your stores. When new vouchers are sold or when they are redeemed (or partially redeemed), e-Comms will pass the data to your other stores.
Note that you will require unique voucher number sequences at each store.
It is strongly advised that you consult Barcode Solutions before setting up a multi-store voucher system.

Title synchronisation options
These checkboxes are used to prevent certain title master fields being updated when synchronising product information. If you select "No abstracts" or "No blurbs/reviews", the data from those fields will not be dispatched to other stores. If the data has been sent by other stores, it will be ignored.
Ticking the other checkboxes ("No prices" etc) will only cause e-Comms to ignore the relevant data sent from other stores. It will still send that data. So you will need to set these options at each store.

Head office set ups
These options are only to be used if you are using central debtors and/or central rewards. In any other multi-store set up they should be left unticked.
It is strongly advised that you consult Barcode Solutions before setting up a multi-store head office system.

Centralised debtors
The setting allows for a head office to control all the debtors for the group. e-Comms is then used to transfer sales history from branch stores to the head office.

Centralised rewards
The setting allows for a head office to control all the customer rewards for the group. e-Comms is then used to transfer sales history from branch stores to the head office.

Installation is head office
Tick this box if this branch is the head office.

Installation has a head office
If another branch acts as a head office, check this box. Then select the head office store code from the drop-down box.

Customer synchronisation
Select the appropriate level of customer synchronisation you require. If you select customer synchronisation, you will first need to append the two character store code to the end of each customer code. There is an option to do this in e-Comms. It is strongly advised that you consult Barcode Solutions before setting Customer synchronisation.

Nielson Bookscan
If you wish to be involved with anonymously sending title sales to Nielson Bookscan for statistical purposes, tick this.
(For book trade only)
Check the options to disable, uncheck to enable.

	        Return to System Options Summary

.topic 187

Adding a new course

Click on the Add Course (F3) button.
In the Code field enter a new course code (up to 10 characters). Note that this field must be unique.
Then fill in the other fields as outlined below:
Course	Course name ( up to 30 characters) . Compulsory field.
Optional fields
Year	The 2 digit year of the course e.g. for 2010 enter 10.
Faculty	10 characters
Department	10 characters
Comments 	Two lines of 50 characters.
Co-ordinators	25 characters
Email	Faculty email ( email and phone numbers for individual lecturers are stored elsewhere).
Phone	Faculty phone number
Terms 1 - 4	Terms (or semesters) that  the course is taught in.
Summer Term (5) If there is a summer term for the course it is stored as term 5.
		
Click on the Save Changes (F12) button so save your changes or Cancel Changes ( use the Escape key) to discard them.
To edit course details, click on the Edit Course (F4) button.	
Note that text in these fields are coloured blue when you are adding or editing them.

Entering historical details
When the course is being set up, you may enter as much historical information as you prefer.
From that point forward, each time the course information is rolled over, the existing data will move one column to the right.
To enter this data manually, click on the Edit Rolls button and type in the numbers to the appropriate fields.
These fields are:
Estimated	The estimate of the number of students taking the course
Pre-enrol	The  the number of students who have pre-enrolled.
March 	If you are using a yearly roll, this is the actual number of students taking the first semester.
July	If you are using a yearly roll, this is the actual number of students taking the second semester.







.topic 122
Adding Pictures To A Report

Once your report has been loaded into the report-designer, select Drawing Tools (from the Tools menu).  Continue the menu-selection with Picture.  Drag out a suitably-sized rectangle onto the report, using this tool, and when you release the mouse-button you will be presented with a dialogue enabling you to identify the picture's sort and whereabouts.  The currently accepted picture-file formats are:  BMP, PCX, PNG, JPG and TGA

More details are available within ReportPro's Help, under "Drawing Tools".
